**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/plans/SF202610310003/recalc-indicators
Idempotency-Key: 9c8d7e6f-5a4b-3c2d-1e0f-9a8b7c6d5e4f
```

```json
{
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "node_codes": ["M1", "M3", "M6"],
  "calc_version": "V1",
  "reason": "术后检查数据补录后重算"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "SF202610310003",
    "eye_side": "OD",
    "recalculated": [
      {"node_code": "M1", "efficacy_corrected": 1.0, "safety_index": 1.0, "stability_valid": false, "pred_abs_delta": 0.25},
      {"node_code": "M3", "efficacy_corrected": 1.2, "safety_index": 1.0, "stability_valid": true, "pred_abs_delta": 0.25}
    ],
    "updated_count": 2,
    "calc_version": "V1",
    "plan_summary_updated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等；2) 校验计划存在与眼别；3) 逐节点执行四项指标计算（见 8.5 公式）；4) **有效样本前置条件**逐项检查并写排除原因；5) 按 `(plan_id, eye_side, node_code, calc_version)` 唯一索引执行 UPSERT（同口径覆盖，换口径并存）；6) 回填计划的 `last_*` 汇总字段与 `indicator_updated_at`；7) 记录 `calc_source = 'RECALC'`；8) 重算为**幂等操作**，可重复执行得到相同结果。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`"1106039"`、`40001`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等；2) 计划存在（`"1106001"`）、状态非终止（`"1106019"`）；3) `description` ≥ 10 字（`"1106034"`）；4) 有数值必须有单位（`"1106003"`）；5) **`GRADE_3`/`GRADE_4` 必须由医师确认**（`confirmed_by`/`confirmed_at` 非空），技师提交时返回 `"1106035"`；6) 服务端计算 `delta_value = measured_value − baseline_value`；7) 生成 `observation_no`（GC），写 `m11_postop_observation`（`status = 'OPEN'`）；8) 返回升级阈值文案与当前差距，供页面提示；9) 发布 `m11.postop.observation.created` 事件。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`"1106003"`、`"1106019"`、`"1106034"`、`"1106035"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-23 观察线索列表

```
GET /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/observations?statuses=OPEN,OBSERVING&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",

      {

        "complication_type": "HASE",

        "delta_value": 1.5,

        "status_text": "待处理",

    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 走部分索引 `idx_m11_postop_observation_open`（`status IN ('OPEN','OBSERVING')`）；2) 支持按观察类型、严重程度、眼别、术后天数区间、节点筛选；3) 按 `severity_grade` 倒序（GRADE_4 优先）再按 `days_after_surgery DESC` 排序；4) `summary` 返回各状态计数与 GRADE_3 及以上数量；5) 手机号脱敏。**错误码：** `"1106030"`、`40300`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-24 转并发症管理

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/observations/GC202611050001/refer
Idempotency-Key: 6f5e4d3c-2b1a-0f9e-8d7c-6b5a4f3e2d1c
```

```json
{
  "complication_type": "HASE",
  "severity_grade": "GRADE_3",
  "is_related_to_surgery": "RELATED",
  "refer_basis": "Haze 已达 2 级并持续超过 90 天，视轴区受累，符合升级标准",
  "need_approval": true,
  "approval_no": "SP202611050003"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "observation_no": "GC202611050001",
    "status": "REFERRED",
    "complication_type": "HASE",
    "severity_grade": "GRADE_3",
    "complication_ref_no": "BZ202611050001",
    "referred_at": "2026-11-05T14:00:00",
    "approval_status": "APPROVED",
    "notified_systems": ["M11-07", "M22"]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等；2) 线索存在且非 `REFERRED`（`"1106036"`）；3) `refer_basis` ≥ 10 字（`"1106037"`）；4) 并发症类型在共享规范 13 节枚举内；5) `need_approval = true` 时必须有已审批通过的 `approval_no`（M20），否则 `"1106003"`；6) 调 M11-07 创建并发症报告（`POST /internal/v1/m11/quality/complications`）并取回 `complication_ref_no`；7) **回填** `complication_ref_no` / `referred_at`，置 `status = 'REFERRED'`、`refer_to_complication = TRUE`；8) 同步 `severity_grade` 与 `is_related_to_surgery`；9) 发布 `m11.postop.observation.referred` 事件；10) M11-07 不可用时返回 502 且**不修改本源线索状态**（保证原子性）。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`"1106003"`、`"1106036"`、`"1106037"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-25 标记观察消退 / 关闭

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/observations/GC202611010001/resolve
```

```json
{
  "target_status": "RESOLVED",
  "final_value": 0.5,
  "resolve_note": "Haze 降至 0.5 级，激素已减量维持，视轴清晰"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "observation_no": "GC202611010001",
    "status": "RESOLVED",
    "resolved_at": "2026-11-20T10:00:00",
    "resolved_days": 19,
    "final_value": 0.5,
    "during_days": 19
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 线索必须 `OPEN`/`OBSERVING`（`"1106019"`）；2) `target_status` 只能是 `RESOLVED` 或 `CLOSED`；3) `resolve_note` ≥ 5 字（`"1106003"`）；4) 置状态与 `resolved_at`，计算 `resolved_days = resolved_at::date − created_at::date`；5) `RESOLVED` 表示异常消退（可作为疗效观察数据），`CLOSED` 表示无需继续跟踪；6) 若该线索是并发症的线索源且已转出，则只允许 `CLOSED`（结论由 M11-07 报告决定）；7) 记录处置轨迹。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`"1106003"`、`"1106019"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

**响应**

```json
        "priority": "NORMAL"
      },
      {
        "task_id": 990021,
        "plan_no": "SF202609010007",
        "patient_name": "陈同学",
        "patient_phone_masked": "137****9012",
        "node_code": "M3",
        "due_date": "2026-12-15",
        "days_to_due": -8,
        "overdue_days": 8,
        "remind_count": 3,

      }
    ],
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 走 `idx_m11_postop_task_due` 与 `idx_m11_postop_task_overdue`；2) 三类合并：即将到期（`days_to_due >= 0` 且在提醒窗口内）、已逾期（`overdue_days > 0`）、待召回（失访且逾期 > 召回启动天数）；3) `priority` 计算：`HIGH` = 逾期且 `reply_intent IN ('CANNOT_COME','REFUSE')` 或 `remind_count >= max`；`NORMAL` = 其余；4) `remind_count` / `last_remind_at` / `reply_intent` 通过 `m11_postop_remind` 聚合（子查询 + `idx_m11_postop_remind_plan`）；5) 手机号脱敏。**错误码：** `40300`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-27 发送随访提醒 / 催告 / 召回

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/remind
Idempotency-Key: 0a9b8c7d-6e5f-4a3b-2c1d-0e9f8a7b6c5d
```

```json
{
  "plan_nos": ["SF202610240011", "SF202609010007"],
  "remind_type": "NODE_DUE",
  "notify_channel": "SMS",
  "template_code": "M11_POSTOP_NODE_DUE"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "success_count": 2,
    "failed": [],
    "items": [
      {
        "plan_no": "SF202610240011",
        "node_code": "W1",
        "remind_id": 660001,
        "remind_type": "NODE_DUE",
        "remind_seq": 2,
        "notify_channel": "SMS",
        "notify_target": "139****5678",
        "send_status": "SENT",
        "provider_msg_id": "MSG20261103000128",
        "send_at": "2026-11-03T09:00:05"
      }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等；2) 逐计划校验：状态 `PLANNED`/`ACTIVE`（`"1106019"`）；3) **提醒次数上限**：`remind_seq = 该计划该节点该类型已有最大序号 + 1`，超过 `max_remind_times`（默认 3）返回 `"1106031"`；4) 写 `m11_postop_remind`（`(plan_id, node_code, remind_type, remind_seq)` 唯一索引防重复）；5) 从计划解密手机号，调 M03-05 `POST /internal/v1/message/send`（API-32），载荷含模板编码与参数（`node_label` / `due_date` / `overdue_days` / `phone_contact`）；6) 回写 `send_status` / `provider_msg_id` / `send_at`；7) `notify_target` **脱敏存储**；8) 成功发送后任务置 `NOTIFIED`；9) M03-05 失败时 `send_status = 'FAILED'` 并进 Celery 重试（最多 3 次），不全量失败；10) 单次最多 100 例（`"1106032"`）。**错误码：** `"1106019"`、`"1106031"`、`"1106032"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

```json
    },
    "visit": {
      "visit_no": "GF202611010002",
      "node_code": "D1",
      "node_label": "术后 1 天",
      "visit_date": "2026-11-01",
      "days_after_surgery": 1,
      "doctor_name": "张医生"
    },
    "baseline": {
      "ucva_decimal": 0.05,
      "bcva_decimal": 1.0,
      "se": -6.0,
      "thickness": 520.0,
      "target_se": 0.0
    },
    "measurements": [
      {
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "ucva_decimal": 1.0,
        "ucva_logmar": 0.0,
        "bcva_decimal": 1.0,
        "sphere": -0.25,
        "cylinder": -0.5,
        "axis": 180,
        "se": -0.5,
        "iop": 22.5,
        "central_thickness": 485.0,
        "flap_thickness": 120.0,
        "residual_stroma": 365.0,
        "haze_grade": 1.5
      }
    ],
    "indicators": [],
    "curve_svg_key": null,
    "advice": {
      "exam_summary": "角膜瓣对位良好，右眼轻度角膜上皮下雾状混浊",
      "doctor_advice": "继续点眼，两周后复查，避免揉眼与游泳",
      "medication": ["0.1%氟米龙 qid 14天"],
      "next_visit": "按计划 W1（2026-11-07）"
    },
    "qr_code": "M11POSTOP|GF202611010002|8f3a2b1c",
    "footer": {
      "signature_name": "张医生",
      "printed_at": "2026-11-01T10:35:00",
      "printed_by": "李技师",
      "is_reprint": false
    }
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) `template` 支持 `VISIT_REPORT`（单次到访）/ `SUMMARY_REPORT`（全周期总结带曲线）/ `INDICATOR_REPORT`（四项指标）/ `DOCTOR_NOTE`（医师小结）；2) `VISIT_REPORT` 需 `visit_no` 参数（`"1106002"`）；3) `SUMMARY_REPORT` 需 ≥ 2 次到访（`"1106038"`）；4) `INDICATOR_REPORT` 需至少 1 条有效指标（`"1106039"`）；5) 二维码 `M11POSTOP|<单号>|<HMAC-SHA256 前 8 位>`；6) 曲线由前端用 ECharts 渲染为 SVG 嵌入；7) 打印后写审计日志（M02-06）；8) 二次打印 `is_reprint = true` 并加水印；9) 批量上限 200 份（`"1106033"`）。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`"1106002"`、`"1106033"`、`"1106038"`、`"1106039"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否。

#### API-33 依从性报表导出

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m11/postop-followups/statistics/export
Idempotency-Key: 7c6b5a4f-3e2d-1c0b-9a8f-7e6d5c4b3a2f
```

```json
{
  "date_from": "2026-01-01",
  "date_to": "2026-10-31",
  "store_id": 201,
  "refractive_types": ["SMILE", "FS_LASIK"],
  "include_detail": true,
  "format": "XLSX"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "export_id": "EXP202611080001",
    "status": "PENDING",
    "estimated_rows": 1320,
    "estimated_seconds": 8,
    "down_url": null,
    "expire_hours": 24,
    "message": "导出任务已提交，完成后可在通知中心下载"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等；2) 校验日期跨度 ≤ 366 天（`"1106030"`）；3) 预估行数，超过 10,000 返回 `"1106033"`；4) 提交 Celery 异步任务 `export_adherence_report`，生成 Excel（汇总页 + 按节点页 + 按术式页 + 按医师页 + 明细页）；5) 返回 `export_id`，完成后通知中心可下载（URL 有效期 24h）；6) 记录导出审计（谁在何时导出了什么范围的数据）。**错误码：** `"1106030"`、`"1106033"`、`40300`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

#### API-34 术后到期随访清单（P-12）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m11/postop-followups/due?window_days=7&store_id=201&limit=500
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "as_of_date": "2026-11-01",
    "window_days": 7,
    "items": [
      {
        "task_id": 990001,
        "plan_no": "SF202610310003",
        "patient_id": 10021,
        "patient_name": "王女士",
        "patient_phone_masked": "138****1234",
        "surgery_date": "2026-10-31",
        "refractive_type": "SMILE",
        "eye_side": "OD",
        "node_code": "D1",
        "due_date": "2026-11-01",
        "days_to_due": 0,
        "overdue_days": 0,
        "remind_count": 0,
        "max_remind_times": 2,
        "is_lost": false
      }
    ],
    "total": 24,
    "overdue_count": 3
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **契约 P-12**，调用方 M03-04（随访任务统一调度）、M19（患者运营召回）；2) 查询 `m11_postop_task` 中 `status IN ('PLANNED','NOTIFIED')` 且 `due_date <= CURRENT_DATE + window_days` 的任务（走 `idx_m11_postop_task_due`）；3) 同时包含**已逾期未处理**的任务（`expire_date < CURRENT_DATE`），按 `due_date ASC` 排序；4) 派生 `days_to_due`（可为负）与 `overdue_days`；5) 关联计划取 `lost_to_followup` 标记；6) `max_remind_times` 从节点配置读取，供调用方判断是否该催；7) 手机号脱敏返回；8) SLA **P95 ≤ 200ms**（结果缓存 60 秒，`m11:postop:due:{store}:{window}`）；9) `limit` 上限 1000；10) 只读接口，内网鉴权。**错误码：** `40001`、`"1106030"`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 否（只读）。

#### API-35 随访计划创建通知（P-13）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m11/postop-followups/on-created
Idempotency-Key: 1f2e3d4c-5b6a-7f8e-9d0c-1b2a3f4e5d6c
```

```json
{
  "plan_no": "SF202610310003",
  "patient_id": 10021,
  "surgery_date": "2026-10-31",
  "refractive_type": "SMILE",
  "eye_side": "OD",
  "source_event_id": "evt_3f2a1b0c9d8e7f65",
  "tasks": [
    {"node_code": "D1", "due_date": "2026-11-01", "task_id": 990001},
    {"node_code": "W1", "due_date": "2026-11-07", "task_id": 990002},
    {"node_code": "M1", "due_date": "2026-11-30", "task_id": 990003},
    {"node_code": "M3", "due_date": "2027-01-29", "task_id": 990004},
    {"node_code": "M6", "due_date": "2027-04-29", "task_id": 990005},
    {"node_code": "Y1", "due_date": "2027-10-31", "task_id": 990006},
    {"node_code": "Y2", "due_date": "2028-10-30", "task_id": 990007}
  ]
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "plan_no": "SF202610310003",
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "surgery_date": "2026-10-31",
    "task_count": 7,
    "first_due_date": "2026-11-01",
    "last_due_date": "2028-10-30",
    "is_idempotent": false,
    "created_at": "2026-11-01T09:12:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "01J8F3K2M9XQ7V4N6R8T2W5Y"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) **契约 P-13**，调用方 M03-04、M19；2) **幂等三道防线**：①`Idempotency-Key` Redis 幂等（`m11:idem:postop:on-created:{key}` TTL 24h）；②按 `plan_no` 查询，已存在则返回原计划摘要并置 `is_idempotent: true`；③`source_event_id` 相同的事件视为重复投递，直接幂等返回；3) 校验 `plan_no` 存在（`"1106001"`）且 `surgery_date` / `task_count` 与库内一致，不一致返回 `"1106040"` 的变体（此处返回 `40001` 并告警，避免脏数据静默）；4) 该接口**不修改**随访计划数据，仅作为「计划已创建」的通知出口（M03-04 据此生成统一调度任务、M19 据此建召回名单）；5) SLA **P95 ≤ 100ms**；6) 返回任务时间和首末到期日供调用方排期；7) 内网鉴权（服务间 token + IP 白名单）。**错误码：** `"1106001"`、`40001`。

**支持 `Idempotency-Key`：** 是。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m11/postop.py
"""M11-06 术后随访与视力追踪 —— 对外路由层。"""

from typing import Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.audit import audit_log
from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import success_response
from app.core.security import require_permission
from app.schemas.m11.postop import (
    NodeConfigIn,
    PostopObservationIn,
    PostopRemindIn,
    PostopTaskMissIn,
    PostopTaskQueryIn,
    PostopVisitCreateIn,
    PostopVisitSignIn,
)
from app.services.m11.postop_indicator_service import PostopIndicatorService
from app.services.m11.postop_observe_service import PostopObserveService
from app.services.m11.postop_plan_service import PostopPlanService
from app.services.m11.postop_remind_service import PostopRemindService
from app.services.m11.postop_visit_service import PostopVisitService

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m11/postop-followups",
                   tags=["M11-06 术后随访与视力追踪"])


# ==================== 工作台与统计 ====================

@router.get("/workbench", summary="随访工作台看板数据")
async def get_workbench(
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, description="院区 ID，按数据权限收敛"),
    surgeon_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, description="主刀医师 ID"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回待办卡片、本周概览、节点达成率、今日清单与异常线索。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_workbench(store_id, surgeon_id, user))


@router.get("/statistics/adherence", summary="随访依从性统计")
async def get_adherence_stat(
    date_from: str = Query(..., description="统计开始日 YYYY-MM-DD"),
    date_to: str = Query(..., description="统计结束日 YYYY-MM-DD"),
    store_id: Optional[int] = Query(None, description="院区 ID"),
    refractive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="术式过滤"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:stat")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按手术日期落窗，统计完成率、失访率与提醒有效率。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.get_adherence_stat(date_from, date_to, store_id,
                                     refractive_type, user)
    )


@router.get("/statistics/indicators", summary="四项指标汇总统计")
async def get_indicator_stat(
    date_from: str = Query(..., description="统计开始日"),
    date_to: str = Query(..., description="统计结束日"),
    node_code: str = Query("Y1", description="评估节点 M1/M3/M6/Y1/Y2"),
    refractive_type: Optional[str] = Query(None, description="术式过滤"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:stat")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """聚合有效性、安全性、稳定性、可预测性，附有效样本与排除原因。"""
    svc = PostopIndicatorService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.get_summary(date_from, date_to, node_code,
                              refractive_type, user)
    )


@router.post("/statistics/export", summary="依从性报表导出")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_EXPORT", object_type="m11_postop_plan")
async def export_adherence(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:export")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """提交异步导出任务，返回 export_id 供下载。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.export_adherence(payload, user,
                                   idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    )


# ==================== 计划查询 ====================

@router.get("/plans", summary="随访计划列表")
async def list_plans(
    query: PostopTaskQueryIn = Depends(),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """多条件分页查询，支持仅逾期 / 仅失访筛选。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.list_plans(query, user))


@router.get("/by-patient/{patient_id}", summary="按患者查随访计划")
async def list_by_patient(
    patient_id: int = Path(..., description="患者 ID"),
    include_completed: bool = Query(True, description="是否含已完成计划"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回该患者全部屈光随访计划，双眼同期以 paired_plan_no 互指。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.list_by_patient(patient_id, include_completed, user)
    )


@router.get("/plans/{plan_no}", summary="随访计划详情")
async def get_plan_detail(
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32, description="随访计划号 SF..."),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """主档 + 任务列表 + 到访列表 + 四项指标汇总。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_detail(plan_no, user))


# ==================== 计划与任务管理 ====================

@router.post("/plans", summary="手工补建随访计划")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_PLAN_CREATE", object_type="m11_postop_plan")
async def create_plan(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按术式配置展开节点任务，并调 P-13 通知调度方。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_plan(payload, user, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                              trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/plans/{plan_no}/terminate", summary="终止随访计划")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_PLAN_TERMINATE", object_type="m11_postop_plan")
async def terminate_plan(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:update")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """终止计划并批量取消未完成任务，需原因说明 ≥ 10 字。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.terminate_plan(plan_no, payload, user,
                                 trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.get("/tasks", summary="随访任务列表")
async def list_tasks(
    query: PostopTaskQueryIn = Depends(),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:view")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """按到期日、状态、节点筛选任务，含逾期与剩余天数。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.list_tasks(query, user))


@router.post("/tasks/{task_id}/miss", summary="标记任务逾期未到")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_TASK_MISS", object_type="m11_postop_task")
async def mark_missed(
    payload: PostopTaskMissIn,
    task_id: int = Path(..., description="任务 ID"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:remind")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """登记未到原因与说明，并按连续逾期数判定失访。"""
    svc = PostopRemindService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.mark_missed(task_id, payload, user))


@router.post("/plans/{plan_no}/extra-visit", summary="新增计划外随访")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_EXTRA_VISIT", object_type="m11_postop_task")
async def create_extra_visit(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """生成 EXTRA 任务，不计入依从率分母。"""
    svc = PostopPlanService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_extra_visit(plan_no, payload, user,
                                     idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    )


@router.post("/plans/{plan_no}/lost", summary="确认失访")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_LOST", object_type="m11_postop_plan")
async def confirm_lost(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:remind")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """标记失访并保留召回入口，可选择同时终止计划。"""
    svc = PostopRemindService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.confirm_lost(plan_no, payload, user,
                               idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    )


# ==================== 到访与检查录入 ====================

@router.get("/plans/{plan_no}/visit-context", summary="到访录入上下文")
async def get_visit_context(
    plan_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    node_code: str = Query("D1", description="本次主节点"),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """返回术前基线、可办理节点、必查项与告警规则。"""
    svc = PostopVisitService(db)
    return success_response(await svc.get_visit_context(plan_no, node_code, user))


@router.post("/visits", summary="创建到访记录并录入检查")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_VISIT_CREATE", object_type="m11_postop_visit")
async def create_visit(
    payload: PostopVisitCreateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """服务端计算 logMAR / SE / 剩余基质床，并判定告警生成线索草稿。"""
    svc = PostopVisitService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.create_visit(payload, user, idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.put("/visits/{visit_no}", summary="修改到访记录（草稿）")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_VISIT_UPDATE", object_type="m11_postop_visit")
async def update_visit(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    visit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:create")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """仅草稿可改，按眼别全量替换检查记录。"""
    svc = PostopVisitService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.update_visit(visit_no, payload, user,
                               trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/visits/{visit_no}/sign", summary="签署到访记录")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_VISIT_SIGN", object_type="m11_postop_visit")
async def sign_visit(
    payload: PostopVisitSignIn,
    visit_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    request: Request = None,
    user=Depends(require_permission("m11:postop:sign")),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
):
    """校验必查项齐全后签署，完成任务、重算依从率并触发指标计算。"""
    svc = PostopVisitService(db)
    return success_response(
        await svc.sign_visit(visit_no, payload, user,
                             idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
                             trace_id=request.state.trace_id)
    )


@router.post("/visits/{visit_no}/void", summary="作废到访记录")
@audit_log(event_code="M11_POSTOP_VISIT_VOID", object_type="m11_postop_visit")
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m11/postop_indicator_service.py
"""M11-06 术后随访 —— 四项核心指标计算服务。"""

import logging
import math
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal, ROUND_HALF_UP
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import and_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.models.m11.postop import (
    M11PostopIndicator,
    M11PostopPlan,
    M11PostopVa,
)

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ASSESS_NODES = ("M1", "M3", "M6", "Y1", "Y2")
STABILITY_THRESHOLD = Decimal("0.50")
STABILITY_MIN_INTERVAL = 30
SAFETY_LOSS_LINES = 2
CALC_VERSION = "V1"


def to_logmar(decimal_va: Optional[Decimal]) -> Optional[Decimal]:
    """小数视力换算为 logMAR：logMAR = -log10(VA)。

    Args:
        decimal_va: 小数视力，如 Decimal('0.8')。

    Returns:
        logMAR 值（保留 3 位，ROUND_HALF_UP）；入参为空或 <= 0 时返回 None。
    """
    if decimal_va is None:
        return None
    va = float(decimal_va)
    if va <= 0:
        return None
    return Decimal(str(round(-math.log10(va), 3)))


def quantize(value: Decimal, places: str = "0.001") -> Decimal:
    """按指定精度做 ROUND_HALF_UP 舍入。"""
    return value.quantize(Decimal(places), rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)


class PostopIndicatorService:
    """四项指标计算：有效性 / 安全性 / 稳定性 / 可预测性。

    设计红线：
        1. 有效样本以 *_valid 布尔为准，缺失值一律不填补（不用 0 / 1.0 兜底）；
        2. 每个无效指标必须写 *_excluded_reason；
        3. 聚合时分母为 0 返回 None 而非 0；
        4. D1/W1 不参与计算（屈光未稳定）。
    """

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db

    async def recalc_plan_node(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        eye_side: str,
        node_code: str,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """计算某计划某眼某节点的四项指标。

        Args:
            plan: 随访计划（含术前基线快照）。
            eye_side: 眼别 OD / OS。
            node_code: 评估节点 M1/M3/M6/Y1/Y2。

        Returns:
            指标字典，可直接写入 m11_postop_indicator。
        """
        if node_code not in ASSESS_NODES:
            raise BizError("1106003", "节点 %s 不参与指标计算" % node_code)

        current = await self._get_va(plan.id, eye_side, node_code)
        previous = await self._get_previous_va(plan.id, eye_side, node_code)

        result: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "plan_id": plan.id,
            "plan_no": plan.plan_no,
            "patient_id": plan.patient_id,
            "eye_side": eye_side,
            "node_code": node_code,
            "days_after_surgery": (current.days_after_surgery
                                   if current is not None else 0),
            "calc_version": CALC_VERSION,
            "calc_at": datetime.now(),
            "calc_source": "AUTO",
            "input_va_id": current.id if current is not None else None,
            "prev_va_id": previous.id if previous is not None else None,
        }
        result.update(self._calc_efficacy(plan, current))
        result.update(self._calc_safety(plan, current))
        result.update(self._calc_stability(plan, current, previous))
        result.update(self._calc_predictability(plan, current))
        return result

    # ==================== 指标一：有效性 ====================

    def _calc_efficacy(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        current: Optional[M11PostopVa],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """有效性 = 术后 UCVA / 术前 BCVA（保留 3 位小数）。"""
        base: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "efficacy_corrected": None,
            "efficacy_uncorrected": None,
            "efficacy_numerator": None,
            "efficacy_denominator": None,
            "efficacy_valid": False,
            "efficacy_excluded_reason": None,
        }
        if current is None or not current.is_valid:
            base["efficacy_excluded_reason"] = "INVALID_MEASURE"
            return base

        postop_ucva = current.ucva_decimal
        if postop_ucva is None:
            base["efficacy_excluded_reason"] = "NO_POSTOP_UCVA"
            return base

        # 分母：术前 BCVA，为空则不计算（严禁填补）
        preop_bcva = plan.preop_bcva_decimal
        if preop_bcva is None or preop_bcva <= 0:
            base["efficacy_excluded_reason"] = "NO_PREOP_BCVA"
            return base

        corrected = quantize(postop_ucva / preop_bcva)
        base["efficacy_numerator"] = postop_ucva
        base["efficacy_denominator"] = preop_bcva
        base["efficacy_corrected"] = corrected
        base["efficacy_valid"] = True

        # 未矫正有效性：分母为术前 UCVA，同样不为空才计算
        preop_ucva = plan.preop_ucva_decimal
        if preop_ucva is not None and preop_ucva > 0:
            base["efficacy_uncorrected"] = quantize(postop_ucva / preop_ucva)
        return base

    # ==================== 指标二：安全性 ====================

    def _calc_safety(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        current: Optional[M11PostopVa],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """安全性 = 术后 BCVA / 术前 BCVA；< 1 表示视力丢失。"""
        base: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "safety_index": None,
            "safety_numerator": None,
            "safety_denominator": None,
            "safety_lines_lost": None,
            "safety_loss_flag": False,
            "safety_valid": False,
            "safety_excluded_reason": None,
        }
        if current is None or not current.is_valid:
            base["safety_excluded_reason"] = "INVALID_MEASURE"
            return base

        postop_bcva = current.bcva_decimal
        if postop_bcva is None:
            base["safety_excluded_reason"] = "NO_POSTOP_BCVA"
            return base

        preop_bcva = plan.preop_bcva_decimal
        if preop_bcva is None or preop_bcva <= 0:
            base["safety_excluded_reason"] = "NO_PREOP_BCVA"
            return base

        index = quantize(postop_bcva / preop_bcva)
        pre_logmar = to_logmar(preop_bcva)
        post_logmar = to_logmar(postop_bcva)
        lines_lost = None
        if pre_logmar is not None and post_logmar is not None:
            # 行数 = (术前 logMAR - 术后 logMAR) / 0.1，正值表示丢失
            lines_lost = quantize((pre_logmar - post_logmar) / Decimal("0.1"),
                                  "0.01")

        base["safety_numerator"] = postop_bcva
        base["safety_denominator"] = preop_bcva
        base["safety_index"] = index
        base["safety_lines_lost"] = lines_lost
        base["safety_valid"] = True
        if lines_lost is not None and lines_lost >= SAFETY_LOSS_LINES:
            base["safety_loss_flag"] = True
        return base

    # ==================== 指标三：稳定性 ====================

    def _calc_stability(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        current: Optional[M11PostopVa],
        previous: Optional[M11PostopVa],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """稳定性 = abs(本次 SE - 上次 SE)，<= 0.50D 视为稳定。"""
        base: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "stability_delta": None,
            "stability_prev_se": None,
            "stability_curr_se": None,
            "stability_prev_days": None,
            "stability_interval_days": None,
            "stability_stable": None,
            "stability_valid": False,
            "stability_excluded_reason": None,
        }
        if current is None or not current.is_refraction_complete:
            base["stability_excluded_reason"] = "DATA_INCOMPLETE"
            return base
        if previous is None or not previous.is_refraction_complete:
            # 首个评估点没有参照，不属于「不稳定」
            base["stability_excluded_reason"] = "NO_PREVIOUS_REFRACTION"
            return base

        interval = current.days_after_surgery - previous.days_after_surgery
        if interval < STABILITY_MIN_INTERVAL:
            base["stability_excluded_reason"] = "INTERVAL_TOO_SHORT"
            return base

        delta = quantize(abs(current.se - previous.se), "0.01")
        base.update({
            "stability_delta": delta,
            "stability_prev_se": previous.se,
            "stability_curr_se": current.se,
            "stability_prev_days": previous.days_after_surgery,
            "stability_interval_days": interval,
            "stability_valid": True,
        })
        # 判定阈值比较使用未舍入原始值，避免 0.504 舍入成 0.50 被误判稳定
        base["stability_stable"] = abs(current.se - previous.se) <= STABILITY_THRESHOLD
        return base

    # ==================== 指标四：可预测性 ====================

    def _calc_predictability(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        current: Optional[M11PostopVa],
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """可预测性 = 术后 SE 是否落在目标 SE ± 容差内。"""
        base: Dict[str, Any] = {
            "pred_actual_se": None,
            "pred_target_se": None,
            "pred_delta": None,
            "pred_abs_delta": None,
            "pred_within_050": False,
            "pred_within_100": False,
            "pred_valid": False,
            "pred_excluded_reason": None,
        }
        if current is None or not current.is_refraction_complete or current.se is None:
            base["pred_excluded_reason"] = "NO_REFRACTION"
            return base

        target = plan.target_se
        if target is None:
            base["pred_excluded_reason"] = "NO_TARGET_SE"
            return base

        delta = quantize(current.se - target, "0.01")
        abs_delta = abs(delta)
        base.update({
            "pred_actual_se": current.se,
            "pred_target_se": target,
            "pred_delta": delta,
            "pred_abs_delta": abs_delta,
            "pred_within_050": abs_delta <= plan.target_se_tolerance_050,
            "pred_within_100": abs_delta <= plan.target_se_tolerance_100,
            "pred_valid": True,
        })
        return base

    # ==================== 内部辅助 ====================

    async def _get_va(
        self,
        plan_id: int,
        eye_side: str,
        node_code: str,
    ) -> Optional[M11PostopVa]:
        """取指定节点的有效视力记录。"""
        return await self.db.scalar(
            select(M11PostopVa).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopVa.plan_id == plan_id,
                    M11PostopVa.eye_side == eye_side,
                    M11PostopVa.node_code == node_code,
                    M11PostopVa.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            ).order_by(M11PostopVa.id.desc()).limit(1)
        )

    async def _get_previous_va(
        self,
        plan_id: int,
        eye_side: str,
        node_code: str,
    ) -> Optional[M11PostopVa]:
        """取该节点之前最近一次有效的屈光度记录（用于稳定性）。"""
        current = await self._get_va(plan_id, eye_side, node_code)
        if current is None:
            return None
        return await self.db.scalar(
            select(M11PostopVa).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopVa.plan_id == plan_id,
                    M11PostopVa.eye_side == eye_side,
                    M11PostopVa.days_after_surgery < current.days_after_surgery,
                    M11PostopVa.is_valid.is_(True),
                    M11PostopVa.is_refraction_complete.is_(True),
                    M11PostopVa.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            ).order_by(M11PostopVa.days_after_surgery.desc()).limit(1)
        )

    async def upsert_indicator(
        self,
        values: Dict[str, Any],
    ) -> M11PostopIndicator:
        """按 (plan, eye, node, version) 唯一键写入或更新指标。"""
        existing = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M11PostopIndicator).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopIndicator.plan_id == values["plan_id"],
                    M11PostopIndicator.eye_side == values["eye_side"],
                    M11PostopIndicator.node_code == values["node_code"],
                    M11PostopIndicator.calc_version == values["calc_version"],
                    M11PostopIndicator.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            )
        )
        if existing is None:
            row = M11PostopIndicator(**values)
            self.db.add(row)
            await self.db.flush()
            return row
        for key, value in values.items():
            setattr(existing, key, value)
        existing.calc_source = "RECALC"
        await self.db.flush()
        return existing

    async def refresh_plan_summary(self, plan_id: int) -> None:
        """把最近一次评估节点的指标回填到计划汇总字段。"""
        plan = await self.db.get(M11PostopPlan, plan_id)
        if plan is None:
            return
        latest = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M11PostopIndicator).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopIndicator.plan_id == plan_id,
                    M11PostopIndicator.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            ).order_by(M11PostopIndicator.days_after_surgery.desc()).limit(1)
        )
        if latest is None:
            return
        plan.last_assess_node = latest.node_code
        plan.last_eff_corrected = latest.efficacy_corrected
        plan.last_eff_uncorrected = latest.efficacy_uncorrected
        plan.last_safety_index = latest.safety_index
        plan.last_stability_delta = latest.stability_delta
        plan.last_stability_stable = latest.stability_stable
        plan.last_predictability_rate = Decimal(
            "1.0") if latest.pred_within_050 else Decimal("0.0")
        plan.indicator_updated_at = datetime.now()
        await self.db.flush()

    async def get_summary(
        self,
        date_from: date,
        date_to: date,
        node_code: str,
        refractive_type: Optional[str],
        user: Any,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """聚合四项指标，分母为 0 时返回 None 而非 0。"""
        filters = [
            M11PostopIndicator.is_deleted.is_(False),
            M11PostopIndicator.node_code == node_code,
            M11PostopPlan.surgery_date >= date_from,
            M11PostopPlan.surgery_date <= date_to,
        ]
        if refractive_type:
            filters.append(M11PostopPlan.refractive_type == refractive_type)

        rows = (await self.db.execute(
            select(M11PostopIndicator).join(
                M11PostopPlan, M11PostopPlan.id == M11PostopIndicator.plan_id
            ).where(and_(*filters))
        )).scalars().all()

        eff_valid = [r for r in rows if r.efficacy_valid]
        safety_valid = [r for r in rows if r.safety_valid]
        stab_valid = [r for r in rows if r.stability_valid]
        pred_valid = [r for r in rows if r.pred_valid]

        def _rate(count: int, total: int) -> Optional[float]:
            """比例计算：分母为 0 返回 None。"""
            if total == 0:
                return None
            return round(count / total, 4)

        return {
            "date_from": date_from,
            "date_to": date_to,
            "node_code": node_code,
            "calc_version": CALC_VERSION,
            "efficacy": {
                "valid_count": len(eff_valid),
                "excluded_count": len(rows) - len(eff_valid),
                "mean_corrected": (float(sum(
                    (r.efficacy_corrected for r in eff_valid), Decimal("0"))
                    / len(eff_valid)) if eff_valid else None),
            },
            "safety": {
                "valid_count": len(safety_valid),
                "excluded_count": len(rows) - len(safety_valid),
                "loss_2lines_count": sum(1 for r in safety_valid if r.safety_loss_flag),
                "loss_2lines_rate": _rate(
                    sum(1 for r in safety_valid if r.safety_loss_flag),
                    len(safety_valid)),
            },
            "stability": {
                "valid_count": len(stab_valid),
                "excluded_count": len(rows) - len(stab_valid),
                "stable_rate": _rate(
                    sum(1 for r in stab_valid if r.stability_stable),
                    len(stab_valid)),
            },
            "predictability": {
                "valid_count": len(pred_valid),
                "excluded_count": len(rows) - len(pred_valid),
                "within_050_rate": _rate(
                    sum(1 for r in pred_valid if r.pred_within_050),
                    len(pred_valid)),
                "within_100_rate": _rate(
                    sum(1 for r in pred_valid if r.pred_within_100),
                    len(pred_valid)),
            },
        }
```

```python
# app/services/m11/postop_visit_service.py
"""M11-06 术后随访 —— 到访登记与签署服务。"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from sqlalchemy import and_, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.clients.m11 import m03_message_client, m10_postop_writer
from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.core.m11.vision import to_logmar
from app.models.m11.postop import (
    M11PostopCornea,
    M11PostopObservation,
    M11PostopPlan,
    M11PostopTask,
    M11PostopVa,
    M11PostopVisit,
)
from app.schemas.m11.postop import PostopVisitCreateIn, PostopVisitSignIn
from app.services.m11.postop_indicator_service import PostopIndicatorService
from app.tasks.m11.postop_tasks import calc_indicators_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

ASSESS_NODES = ("M1", "M3", "M6", "Y1", "Y2")
ADHERENCE_GOOD = Decimal("0.80")
ADHERENCE_FAIR = Decimal("0.50")


class PostopVisitService:
    """到访登记、检查录入、签署与作废。"""

    def __init__(self, db: AsyncSession) -> None:
        self.db = db
        self.indicators = PostopIndicatorService(db)

    async def create_visit(
        self,
        payload: PostopVisitCreateIn,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """创建到访并录入检查数据。"""
        plan = await self._get_plan(payload.plan_no)
        if plan.status not in ("PLANNED", "ACTIVE"):
            raise BizError("1106019", "计划状态不允许录入随访")

        if payload.visit_date < plan.surgery_date:
            raise BizError("1106011", "到访日期不得早于手术日期")
        if payload.visit_date > date.today():
            raise BizError("1106011", "到访日期不得晚于今天")
        if payload.primary_node_code not in payload.covered_nodes:
            raise BizError("1106003", "主节点必须包含在覆盖节点中")

        tasks = await self._get_open_tasks(plan.id, payload.covered_nodes)
        if not tasks:
            raise BizError("1106019", "本次到访的节点均已处理或不可办理")

        days_after = (payload.visit_date - plan.surgery_date).days
        visit_no = await self._next_no("GF")
        visit = M11PostopVisit(
            visit_no=visit_no,
            plan_id=plan.id,
            plan_no=plan.plan_no,
            patient_id=plan.patient_id,
            patient_name=plan.patient_name,
            visit_date=payload.visit_date,
            visit_time=datetime.now(),
            primary_node_code=payload.primary_node_code,
            covered_nodes=payload.covered_nodes,
            covered_task_count=len(tasks),
            days_after_surgery=days_after,
            visit_type=payload.visit_type,
            doctor_id=payload.doctor_id,
            examiner_id=payload.examiner_id,
            exam_summary=payload.exam_summary,
            doctor_advice=payload.doctor_advice,
            medication_given=payload.medication_given,
            treatment_given=payload.treatment_given,
            diagnosis_codes=payload.diagnosis_codes,
            referral_type=payload.referral_type,
            status="DRAFT",
            created_by=user.id,
        )
        self.db.add(visit)
        await self.db.flush()

        va_ids: List[int] = []
        computed: Dict[str, Any] = {"ucva_logmar": {}, "se": {},
                                    "residual_stroma": {}}
        for row in payload.va_records:
            data = row.to_row()
            va = M11PostopVa(
                visit_id=visit.id,
                plan_id=plan.id,
                patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                eye_side=data["eye_side"],
                node_code=payload.primary_node_code,
                days_after_surgery=days_after,
                measure_date=payload.visit_date,
                ucva_decimal=data["ucva_decimal"],
                ucva_logmar=data["ucva_logmar"],
                ucva_method=data["ucva_method"],
                bcva_decimal=data["bcva_decimal"],
                bcva_logmar=data["bcva_logmar"],
                sphere=data["sphere"],
                cylinder=data["cylinder"],
                axis=data["axis"],
                se=data["se"],
                refraction_method=data["refraction_method"],
                iop=data["iop"],
                iop_method=data["iop_method"],
                pupil_size=data["pupil_size"],
                is_valid=data["is_valid"],
                invalid_reason=data["invalid_reason"],
                is_refraction_complete=data["is_refraction_complete"],
                want_buffer=data["want_buffer"],
                remark=data["remark"],
                created_by=user.id,
            )
            self.db.add(va)
            await self.db.flush()
            va_ids.append(va.id)
            computed["ucva_logmar"][data["eye_side"]] = (
                float(data["ucva_logmar"]) if data["ucva_logmar"] else None)
            computed["se"][data["eye_side"]] = (
                float(data["se"]) if data["se"] else None)

        cornea_ids: List[int] = []
        for row in payload.cornea_records or []:
            cornea = M11PostopCornea(
                visit_id=visit.id,
                plan_id=plan.id,
                patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                eye_side=row.eye_side,
                node_code=payload.primary_node_code,
                days_after_surgery=days_after,
                measure_date=payload.visit_date,
                central_thickness=(Decimal(str(row.central_thickness))
                                   if row.central_thickness else None),
                thinnest_thickness=(Decimal(str(row.thinnest_thickness))
                                    if row.thinnest_thickness else None),
                flap_thickness=(Decimal(str(row.flap_thickness))
                                if row.flap_thickness else None),
                residual_stroma=row.computed_residual_stroma(),
                thickness_device=row.thickness_device,
                haze_grade=(Decimal(str(row.haze_grade))
                            if row.haze_grade is not None else None),
                haze_grade_label=self._haze_label(row.haze_grade),
                haze_extent=row.haze_extent,
                topo_report_no=row.topo_report_no,
                topo_k1=(Decimal(str(row.topo_k1)) if row.topo_k1 else None),
                topo_k2=(Decimal(str(row.topo_k2)) if row.topo_k2 else None),
                topo_kmax=(Decimal(str(row.topo_kmax)) if row.topo_kmax else None),
                tbut=(Decimal(str(row.tbut)) if row.tbut is not None else None),
                healing_status=row.healing_status,
                flap_position=row.flap_position,
                is_abnormal=self._judge_cornea_abnormal(row),
                created_by=user.id,
            )
            self.db.add(cornea)
            await self.db.flush()
            cornea_ids.append(cornea.id)
            computed["residual_stroma"][row.eye_side] = (
                float(cornea.residual_stroma) if cornea.residual_stroma else None)

        # 关联任务置 ARRIVED
        for task in tasks:
            task.status = "ARRIVED"
            task.visit_id = visit.id
            task.completed_items = self._completed_items(payload)
            task.item_completion_rate = self._item_rate(task, payload)
            task.is_data_complete = task.item_completion_rate >= Decimal("1")

        observations = await self._judge_alerts(plan, visit, payload, user)
        await self.db.commit()

        return {
            "visit_no": visit_no,
            "plan_no": plan.plan_no,
            "status": visit.status,
            "days_after_surgery": days_after,
            "covered_nodes": payload.covered_nodes,
            "va_ids": va_ids,
            "cornea_ids": cornea_ids,
            "computed": computed,
            "generated_observations": observations,
            "is_idempotent": False,
            "created_at": visit.created_at,
        }

    async def sign_visit(
        self,
        visit_no: str,
        payload: PostopVisitSignIn,
        user: Any,
        idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None,
        trace_id: Optional[str] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """签署到访：校验必查项、完成任务、重算依从率、触发指标。"""
        visit = await self._get_visit(visit_no, for_update=True)
        if visit.status != "DRAFT":
            raise BizError("1106019", "仅草稿状态可签署")
        if len(payload.doctor_advice.strip()) < 10:
            raise BizError("1106022", "医师意见不少于 10 个字")

        tasks = (await self.db.scalars(
            select(M11PostopTask).where(
                and_(M11PostopTask.visit_id == visit.id,
                     M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )
        )).all()
        incomplete = [t.node_code for t in tasks if not t.is_data_complete]
        if incomplete:
            raise BizError("1106021", "必查项未完成，暂不可签署：%s"
                           % "、".join(incomplete))

        now = datetime.now()
        visit.status = "SIGNED"
        visit.signed_by = user.id
        visit.signed_at = now
        visit.doctor_advice = payload.doctor_advice
        visit.next_visit_advice = payload.next_visit_advice

        completed_ids: List[int] = []
        for task in tasks:
            task.status = "COMPLETED"
            task.completed_at = now
            task.completed_by = user.id
            task.visit_date = visit.visit_date
            task.delay_days = (visit.visit_date - task.plan_date).days
            completed_ids.append(task.id)

        plan = await self._get_plan(visit.plan_no)
        await self._refresh_adherence(plan)

        assess_done = [t.node_code for t in tasks
                       if t.node_code in ASSESS_NODES]
        for node_code in assess_done:
            for eye in ("OD", "OS"):
                va = await self.indicators._get_va(plan.id, eye, node_code)
                if va is not None:
                    calc_indicators_task.delay(plan.id, eye, node_code)

        writeback = {"status": "SKIPPED", "archive_ref": None}
        if payload.write_back_m10:
            try:
                ref = await m10_postop_writer.write_visit(visit)
                visit.m10_written = True
                visit.m10_written_at = now
                plan.m10_sync_status = "SYNCED"
                plan.m10_archive_ref = ref
                plan.m10_synced_at = now
                writeback = {"status": "SYNCED", "archive_ref": ref}
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.error("m10 writeback failed visit=%s err=%s", visit_no, exc)
                plan.m10_sync_status = "FAILED"
                writeback = {"status": "FAILED", "archive_ref": None}

        await self.db.commit()
        return {
            "visit_no": visit.visit_no,
            "status": visit.status,
            "signed_at": visit.signed_at,
            "completed_task_ids": completed_ids,
            "plan_status": plan.status,
            "plan_adherence_rate": (float(plan.adherence_rate)
                                    if plan.adherence_rate else None),
            "indicators_computed": assess_done,
            "m10_writeback": writeback,
            "notified_systems": ["M10-09", "M19"],
        }

    # ==================== 内部辅助 ====================

    async def _get_plan(self, plan_no: str) -> M11PostopPlan:
        """按计划号取计划，不存在抛 1106001。"""
        plan = await self.db.scalar(
            select(M11PostopPlan).where(
                and_(M11PostopPlan.plan_no == plan_no,
                     M11PostopPlan.is_deleted.is_(False))
            )
        )
        if plan is None:
            raise BizError("1106001", "随访计划不存在或已删除")
        return plan

    async def _get_visit(self, visit_no: str, for_update: bool = False) -> M11PostopVisit:
        """按到访单号取记录。"""
        stmt = select(M11PostopVisit).where(
            and_(M11PostopVisit.visit_no == visit_no,
                 M11PostopVisit.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )
        if for_update:
            stmt = stmt.with_for_update()
        visit = await self.db.scalar(stmt)
        if visit is None:
            raise BizError("1106002", "到访记录不存在或已作废")
        return visit

    async def _get_open_tasks(
        self,
        plan_id: int,
        node_codes: List[str],
    ) -> List[M11PostopTask]:
        """取本次到访要处理的未完成任务。"""
        return list((await self.db.scalars(
            select(M11PostopTask).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopTask.plan_id == plan_id,
                    M11PostopTask.node_code.in_(node_codes),
                    M11PostopTask.status.in_(("PLANNED", "NOTIFIED")),
                    M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            ).order_by(M11PostopTask.node_seq)
        )).all())

    async def _refresh_adherence(self, plan: M11PostopPlan) -> None:
        """重算计划依从率与下一到期日。"""
        tasks = (await self.db.scalars(
            select(M11PostopTask).where(
                and_(M11PostopTask.plan_id == plan.id,
                     M11PostopTask.is_extra.is_(False),
                     M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False))
            ).order_by(M11PostopTask.node_seq)
        )).all()

        total = len(tasks)
        completed = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status == "COMPLETED")
        missed = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status == "MISSED")
        plan.planned_task_count = total
        plan.completed_task_count = completed
        plan.missed_task_count = missed
        if total > 0:
            rate = (Decimal(completed) / Decimal(total)).quantize(
                Decimal("0.0001"))
            plan.adherence_rate = rate
            if rate >= ADHERENCE_GOOD:
                plan.adherence_level = "GOOD"
            elif rate >= ADHERENCE_FAIR:
                plan.adherence_level = "FAIR"
            else:
                plan.adherence_level = "POOR"

        pending = [t for t in tasks if t.status in ("PLANNED", "NOTIFIED")]
        if pending:
            nxt = min(pending, key=lambda t: t.due_date)
            plan.next_due_date = nxt.due_date
            plan.next_node_code = nxt.node_code
            if plan.status == "PLANNED":
                plan.status = "ACTIVE"
        else:
            plan.next_due_date = None
            plan.next_node_code = None
            plan.status = "COMPLETED"
        await self.db.flush()

    @staticmethod
    def _haze_label(grade: Optional[float]) -> Optional[str]:
        """Haze 分级中文标签。"""
        if grade is None:
            return None
        mapping = {0: "0 级 无", 0.5: "0.5 级 疑有", 1: "1 级 轻微",
                   1.5: "1.5 级 轻中度", 2: "2 级 中度", 2.5: "2.5 级 中重度",
                   3: "3 级 重度", 3.5: "3.5 级 重极重度", 4: "4 级 极重度"}
        return mapping.get(float(grade))

    @staticmethod
    def _judge_cornea_abnormal(row: Any) -> bool:
        """角膜异常判定：Haze >= 2 或最薄点 < 400μm。"""
        if row.haze_grade is not None and row.haze_grade >= 2:
            return True
        if row.thinnest_thickness is not None and row.thinnest_thickness < 400:
            return True
        return False

    @staticmethod
    def _completed_items(payload: PostopVisitCreateIn) -> List[str]:
        """根据实际录入推导已完成检查项。"""
        items: List[str] = []
        if payload.va_records:
            items.append("UCVA")
            if any(r.bcva_decimal for r in payload.va_records):
                items.append("BCVA")
            if any(r.is_refraction_complete() if hasattr(r, "is_refraction_complete")
                   else (r.sphere is not None and r.cylinder is not None)
                   for r in payload.va_records):
                items.append("REFRACTION")
            if any(r.iop is not None for r in payload.va_records):
                items.append("IOP")
        if payload.cornea_records:
            if any(r.central_thickness is not None for r in payload.cornea_records):
                items.append("CORNEAL_THICKNESS")
            if any(r.haze_grade is not None for r in payload.cornea_records):
                items.append("HAZE")
            if any(r.topo_report_no for r in payload.cornea_records):
                items.append("TOPOGRAPHY")
            if any(r.tbut is not None for r in payload.cornea_records):
                items.append("TBUT")
        return items

    @staticmethod
    def _item_rate(task: M11PostopTask, payload: PostopVisitCreateIn) -> Decimal:
        """计算检查项完成率。"""
        required = set(task.required_items or [])
        done = set(PostopVisitService._completed_items(payload))
        if not required:
            return Decimal("1")
        return (Decimal(len(required & done)) / Decimal(len(required))).quantize(
            Decimal("0.0001"))

    async def _judge_alerts(
        self,
        plan: M11PostopPlan,
        visit: M11PostopVisit,
        payload: PostopVisitCreateIn,
        user: Any,
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """按告警规则生成观察线索草稿。"""
        created: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for va in payload.va_records:
            if va.iop is not None and va.iop > 25.0:
                obs = M11PostopObservation(
                    observation_no=await self._next_no("GC"),
                    plan_id=plan.id,
                    plan_no=plan.plan_no,
                    visit_id=visit.id,
                    patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                    eye_side=va.eye_side,
                    node_code=payload.primary_node_code,
                    days_after_surgery=visit.days_after_surgery,
                    observation_type="IOP_ELEVATED",
                    measured_value=Decimal(str(va.iop)),
                    measured_unit="MMHG",
                    baseline_value=None,
                    delta_value=None,
                    threshold_code="IOP_GT_25",
                    description="眼压 %s mmHg 超过预警阈值 25mmHg" % va.iop,
                    is_related_to_surgery="SUSPECTED",
                    status="OPEN",
                    found_by=user.id,
                    created_by=user.id,
                )
                self.db.add(obs)
                await self.db.flush()
                created.append({"observation_no": obs.observation_no,
                                "observation_type": "IOP_ELEVATED",
                                "measured_value": float(obs.measured_value)})
        for co in payload.cornea_records or []:
            if co.haze_grade is not None and co.haze_grade >= 2.0:
                obs = M11PostopObservation(
                    observation_no=await self._next_no("GC"),
                    plan_id=plan.id,
                    plan_no=plan.plan_no,
                    visit_id=visit.id,
                    patient_id=plan.patient_id,
                    eye_side=co.eye_side,
                    node_code=payload.primary_node_code,
                    days_after_surgery=visit.days_after_surgery,
                    observation_type="HAZE_AGGRAVATION",
                    complication_type="HASE",
                    measured_value=Decimal(str(co.haze_grade)),
                    measured_unit="GRADE",
                    threshold_code="HAZE_GE_2",
                    description="Haze %s 级达到或超过预警阈值 2 级" % co.haze_grade,
                    is_related_to_surgery="RELATED",
                    status="OPEN",
                    found_by=user.id,
                    created_by=user.id,
                )
                self.db.add(obs)
                await self.db.flush()
                created.append({"observation_no": obs.observation_no,
                                "observation_type": "HAZE_AGGRAVATION",
                                "measured_value": float(obs.measured_value)})
        return created

    async def _next_no(self, prefix: str) -> str:
        """生成单据号（Redis INCR，失败降级数据库）。"""
        from app.services.m11.code_service import CodeService
        return await CodeService().next_no(prefix)
```

---

## 7. 缓存与异步任务设计

### 7.1 Redis 缓存设计

| Key 模式 | 类型 | TTL | 说明 | 失效时机 | 容量估算 |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m11:postop:due:{store}:{window}` | String | 60s | **P-12 到期清单**（SLA 最严 P95 ≤ 200ms） | 任务状态变化后 DEL | 门店数 × 40KB = 200KB |
| `m11:postop:workbench:{store}` | String | 60s | P01 看板聚合 | 定时任务 60s 刷新 | 门店数 × 8KB = 40KB |
| `m11:postop:nodecfg:{type}` | String | 1h | 节点配置（常量） | 配置保存后 DEL + 版本广播 | 12 组 × 6KB = 72KB |
| `m11:postop:curve:{plan}:{eye}` | String | 120s | P05 曲线数据 | 到访签署 / 作废后 DEL | 200 患者 × 12KB = 2.4MB |
| `m11:postop:plan:detail:{plan_no}` | String | 60s | 计划详情 | 计划写操作后 DEL | 300 × 6KB = 1.8MB |
| `m11:postop:stat:adherence:{store}:{from}:{to}` | String | 10min | P09 依从性报表 | 定时任务每小时预热 | 20 × 40KB = 800KB |
| `m11:postop:stat:indicator:{node}:{from}:{to}` | String | 10min | 四项指标汇总 | 指标重算后 DEL | 20 × 30KB = 600KB |
| `m11:postop:draft:{user_id}:{plan_no}` | Hash | 24h | P04 录入草稿（防误关丢失） | 签署成功 / 手动清除 | 30 × 4KB = 120KB |
| `m11:postop:lock:{plan_no}` | String | 10s | 计划写操作分布式锁 | 操作完成主动释放 | 并发数 × 40B |
| `m11:idem:postop:plan:{key}` | String | 24h | 建计划幂等 | TTL 到期 | 5/日 × 2KB |
| `m11:idem:postop:visit:{key}` | String | 24h | 建到访幂等 | TTL 到期 | 25/日 × 3KB |
| `m11:idem:postop:remind:{key}` | String | 24h | 提醒发送幂等 | TTL 到期 | 10/日 × 1KB |
| `m11:idem:postop:on-created:{key}` | String | 24h | **P-13 幂等** | TTL 到期 | 5/日 × 2KB |
| `m11:version:postop` | String | 永久 | 配置版本号（失效广播） | 配置变更时 INCR | 20B |
| `m11:code:SF:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 随访计划号计数器 | TTL 到期 | 10 × 60B |
| `m11:code:GF:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 到访单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m11:code:GC:{yyyyMMdd}` | String | 3d | 观察单号计数器 | TTL 到期 | — |
| `m11:postop:ws:{store_id}` | Pub/Sub | — | WebSocket 看板广播 | 实时 | — |

**总容量估算：** 约 6.0 MB（常驻），峰值 < 15 MB。Redis 实例按 2 GB 规划，屈光域占比 < 1%。

**缓存一致性策略：**

```
写操作（建计划 / 到访 / 签署 / 作废 / 提醒 / 配置变更）
   │
   ├─► ① 先写数据库（事务提交）
   ├─► ② DEL 相关缓存键（计划详情 / 曲线 / 到期清单 / 看板）
   ├─► ③ 失效指标与统计缓存（stat:*）
   └─► ④ 发 WebSocket 广播刷新前台页面
```

> **为什么不缓存原始检查数据？** `m11_postop_va` / `m11_postop_cornea` 是医疗数据的唯一真值，
> 任何缓存副本都可能与库不一致，而随访报告的打印数据必须与库严格一致（举证要求）。
> 因此只缓存**聚合结果**（曲线、看板、统计），原始记录一律走库，靠索引保证 P95。

### 7.2 Celery 异步任务

```python
# app/tasks/m11/postop_tasks.py
"""M11-06 术后随访 —— Celery 异步任务。"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict, List

from celery import shared_task

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

MAX_RETRY = 3
RETRY_DELAY = 60


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY, default_retry_delay=RETRY_DELAY)
def calc_indicators_task(
    self,
    plan_id: int,
    eye_side: str,
    node_code: str,
) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """计算并落盘某计划某眼某节点的四项指标。"""
    from app.core.database import SyncSessionLocal
    from app.services.m11.postop_indicator_service import PostopIndicatorService

    with SyncSessionLocal() as db:
        try:
            svc = PostopIndicatorService(db)
            plan = db.get_plan(plan_id)
            values = svc.recalc_plan_node_sync(plan, eye_side, node_code)
            row = svc.upsert_indicator(values)
            svc.refresh_plan_summary(plan_id)
            db.commit()
            return {
                "plan_id": plan_id,
                "eye_side": eye_side,
                "node_code": node_code,
                "indicator_id": row.id,
                "calc_version": values["calc_version"],
            }
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            logger.error("calc_indicators_task failed plan=%s eye=%s node=%s err=%s",
                         plan_id, eye_side, node_code, exc)
            db.rollback()
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY, default_retry_delay=RETRY_DELAY)
def notify_plan_created(self, plan_no: str, source_event_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """调用 P-13 通知 M03-04 / M19 计划已创建。"""
    from app.clients.m11 import m03_followup_client, m19_operation_client

    notified: List[str] = []
    errors: List[str] = []
    for name, func in (
        ("M03-04", m03_followup_client.on_postop_plan_created),
        ("M19", m19_operation_client.on_postop_plan_created),
    ):
        try:
            func(plan_no=plan_no, source_event_id=source_event_id)
            notified.append(name)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            errors.append("%s: %s" % (name, exc))

    if len(errors) == 2:
        raise self.retry(exc=RuntimeError("; ".join(errors)))
    logger.info("notify_plan_created plan=%s notified=%s", plan_no, notified)
    return {"plan_no": plan_no, "notified": notified, "errors": errors}


@shared_task(bind=True, max_retries=MAX_RETRY, default_retry_delay=RETRY_DELAY)
def send_remind_task(self, remind_payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """调 M03-05 发送短信 / 微信，回写发送结果。"""
    from app.clients.m11 import m03_message_client
    from app.core.database import SyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopRemind

    with SyncSessionLocal() as db:
        try:
            resp = m03_message_client.send(**remind_payload)
            remind = db.get(M11PostopRemind, remind_payload["remind_id"])
            if remind is not None:
                remind.send_status = "SENT"
                remind.send_at = datetime.now()
                remind.provider_msg_id = resp.get("msg_id")
                db.commit()
            return {"remind_id": remind_payload["remind_id"],
                    "status": "SENT", "msg_id": resp.get("msg_id")}
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            remind = db.get(M11PostopRemind, remind_payload["remind_id"])
            if remind is not None:
                remind.send_status = "FAILED"
                remind.fail_reason = str(exc)[:250]
                remind.retry_count = (remind.retry_count or 0) + 1
                db.commit()
            logger.warning("send_remind_task failed remind=%s err=%s",
                           remind_payload["remind_id"], exc)
            raise self.retry(exc=exc)


@shared_task
def mark_remind_effective() -> Dict[str, int]:
    """标记提醒有效性：发送后 7 天内完成到访的置 is_effective = TRUE。"""
    from app.core.database import SyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopRemind, M11PostopTask

    with SyncSessionLocal() as db:
        cutoff = datetime.now() - timedelta(days=7)
        rows = db.query(M11PostopRemind).filter(
            M11PostopRemind.is_effective.is_(False),
            M11PostopRemind.send_at.isnot(None),
            M11PostopRemind.send_at <= cutoff,
        ).limit(2000).all()
        marked = 0
        for remind in rows:
            task = db.query(M11PostopTask).filter(
                M11PostopTask.plan_id == remind.plan_id,
                M11PostopTask.node_code == remind.node_code,
                M11PostopTask.status == "COMPLETED",
            ).first()
            if task is not None and task.completed_at is not None:
                if task.completed_at <= remind.send_at + timedelta(days=7):
                    remind.is_effective = True
                    remind.effective_at = task.completed_at
                    marked += 1
        db.commit()
    logger.info("mark_remind_effective marked=%d", marked)
    return {"marked": marked}


@shared_task
def writeback_m10_retry() -> Dict[str, int]:
    """重试写回 M10 失败的到访记录。"""
    from app.clients.m11 import m10_postop_writer
    from app.core.database import SyncSessionLocal
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopPlan

    with SyncSessionLocal() as db:
        plans = db.query(M11PostopPlan).filter(
            M11PostopPlan.m10_sync_status == "FAILED",
            M11PostopPlan.is_deleted.is_(False),
        ).limit(200).all()
        fixed = 0
        for plan in plans:
            try:
                ref = m10_postop_writer.write_plan_summary(plan)
                plan.m10_sync_status = "SYNCED"
                plan.m10_archive_ref = ref
                plan.m10_synced_at = datetime.now()
                fixed += 1
            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
                logger.warning("writeback retry failed plan=%s err=%s",
                               plan.plan_no, exc)
        db.commit()
    return {"fixed": fixed}


@shared_task
def export_adherence_report(export_id: str, query: Dict[str, Any],
                            user_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """导出依从性报表 Excel，结果上传 OSS 供下载 24 小时。"""
    from app.services.m11.postop_export_service import PostopExportService

    svc = PostopExportService()
    url = svc.export_adherence(export_id, query, user_id)
    logger.info("export_adherence_report done export_id=%s url=%s", export_id, url)
    return {"export_id": export_id, "url": url}
```

**任务清单：**

| 任务 | 触发 | 说明 | 失败处理 |
|---|---|---|---|
| `calc_indicators_task` | 到访签署（评估节点）/ 手工重算 | 计算四项指标并落盘 | 重试 3 次，仍失败告警并可手工重算 |
| `notify_plan_created` | 建计划后 | 调 P-13 通知调度方 | 重试 3 次，进死信 |
| `send_remind_task` | 提醒发送 | 调 M03-05 | 重试 3 次，`send_status = FAILED` |
| `mark_remind_effective` | 每日 02:10 | 回填提醒有效性 | 幂等可重跑 |
| `writeback_m10_retry` | 每日 03:40 | 重试 M10 写回失败 | 幂等可重跑 |
| `export_adherence_report` | 用户导出 | 生成 Excel | 失败通知用户重试 |

### 7.3 APScheduler 定时任务

| 任务 ID | Cron（Asia/Shanghai） | 说明 | 幂等 | 告警 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `m11_postop_due_remind` | `0 9 * * *` | 每日 09:00 扫描到期前 N 天任务并自动提醒 | 是（`remind_seq` 唯一） | 发送失败率 > 5% |
| `m11_postop_overdue_mark` | `0 1 * * *` | 每日 01:00 将超 `expire_date` 未完成任务置 `MISSED`（自动记原因 `NO_CONTACT` 待人工修正） | 是 | 逾期 > 20 例 |
| `m11_postop_lost_judge` | `30 1 * * *` | 每日 01:30 判定失访（连续 N 评估节点逾期且无到访） | 是 | 新增失访 > 5 例/日 |
| `m11_postop_remind_effective` | `10 2 * * *` | 每日 02:10 回填提醒有效性 | 是 | — |
| `m11_postop_plan_activate` | `0 2 * * *` | 每日 02:00 将进入 D1 窗口的 `PLANNED` 计划置 `ACTIVE` | 是 | — |
| `m11_postop_next_due_refresh` | `20 2 * * *` | 每日 02:20 刷新计划的 `next_due_date` / `next_node_code` | 是 | — |
| `m11_postop_m10_writeback_retry` | `40 3 * * *` | 每日 03:40 重试 M10 写回失败 | 是 | 失败 > 10 条 |
| `m11_postop_indicator_audit` | `0 4 * * *` | 每日 04:00 巡检：评估节点已完成但指标缺失的补算 | 是 | 缺失 > 0 |
| `m11_postop_reconcile` | `20 4 * * *` | 每日 04:20 对账：计划计数 vs 任务聚合、依从率 vs 明细 | 是 | 差异 > 0 |
| `m11_postop_ensure_partition` | `0 4 25 * *` | 每月 25 日 04:00 预建下月提醒分区 | 是 | 失败告警 |
| `m11_postop_stat_preheat` | `50 * * * *` | 每小时预热 P01/P09 统计缓存 | 是 | — |
| `m11_postop_observe_overdue` | `0 8 * * *` | 每日 08:00 扫描超 30 天未处理的观察线索并告警 | 是 | > 5 条告警 |

```python
# app/jobs/m11/postop_jobs.py
"""M11-06 术后随访 —— APScheduler 定时任务。"""

import logging
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, Dict

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

REMIND_BEFORE_DAYS = 3
REMIND_MAX_TIMES = 3
LOST_MISSED_COUNT = 2


async def due_remind_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """扫描即将到期任务，自动发送提醒（受提醒次数上限约束）。

    Args:
        db: 异步会话。
        redis: Redis 客户端。

    Returns:
        统计结果 {"scanned": n, "sent": m, "skipped": k}。
    """
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopPlan, M11PostopTask

    horizon = date.today() + timedelta(days=REMIND_BEFORE_DAYS)
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M11PostopTask.id, M11PostopTask.plan_id, M11PostopTask.node_code,
               M11PostopTask.remind_count, M11PostopTask.due_date).where(
            and_(
                M11PostopTask.status.in_(("PLANNED", "NOTIFIED")),
                M11PostopTask.due_date <= horizon,
                M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )).all()

    sent, skipped = 0, 0
    for task_id, plan_id, node_code, remind_count, due_date in rows:
        if int(remind_count or 0) >= REMIND_MAX_TIMES:
            skipped += 1
            continue
        from app.tasks.m11.postop_tasks import send_remind_task
        send_remind_task.delay({
            "plan_id": plan_id,
            "node_code": node_code,
            "remind_type": "NODE_DUE",
            "channel": "SMS",
        })
        sent += 1

    logger.info("due_remind_job scanned=%d sent=%d skipped=%d",
                len(rows), sent, skipped)
    return {"scanned": len(rows), "sent": sent, "skipped": skipped}


async def overdue_mark_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """将超过失效日仍未完成的任务标记为 MISSED。"""
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopTask

    today = date.today()
    rows = (await db.execute(
        select(M11PostopTask).where(
            and_(
                M11PostopTask.status.in_(("PLANNED", "NOTIFIED", "ARRIVED")),
                M11PostopTask.expire_date < today,
                M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )).scalars().all()

    for task in rows:
        task.status = "MISSED"
        task.missed_reason = "NO_CONTACT"
        task.missed_remark = "系统自动判定逾期未到，待随访人员核实原因"

    await db.commit()
    await redis.set("m11:postop:overdue_count", str(len(rows)), ex=86400)
    logger.info("overdue_mark_job marked=%d", len(rows))
    return {"marked": len(rows)}


async def lost_judge_job(db: Any, redis: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """判定失访：连续 N 个评估节点逾期且期间无到访。"""
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopPlan, M11PostopTask

    plans = (await db.execute(
        select(M11PostopPlan).where(
            and_(
                M11PostopPlan.status == "ACTIVE",
                M11PostopPlan.lost_to_followup.is_(False),
                M11PostopPlan.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        )
    )).scalars().all()

    judged = 0
    for plan in plans:
        tasks = (await db.execute(
            select(M11PostopTask).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopTask.plan_id == plan.id,
                    M11PostopTask.is_extra.is_(False),
                    M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            ).order_by(M11PostopTask.node_seq)
        )).scalars().all()
        consecutive = 0
        for task in tasks:
            if task.status == "COMPLETED":
                consecutive = 0
            elif task.status == "MISSED":
                consecutive += 1
                if consecutive >= LOST_MISSED_COUNT:
                    plan.lost_to_followup = True
                    plan.lost_confirmed_at = datetime.now()
                    judged += 1
                    break
            else:
                break

    await db.commit()
    if judged:
        logger.warning("lost_judge_job newly_lost=%d", judged)
    return {"newly_lost": judged}


async def indicator_audit_job(db: Any) -> Dict[str, int]:
    """巡检：评估节点已完成但指标缺失的补算。"""
    from app.models.m11.postop import (
        M11PostopIndicator,
        M11PostopTask,
        M11PostopVa,
    )

    assess_nodes = ("M1", "M3", "M6", "Y1", "Y2")
    tasks = (await db.execute(
        select(M11PostopTask).where(
            and_(
                M11PostopTask.status == "COMPLETED",
                M11PostopTask.node_code.in_(assess_nodes),
                M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
            )
        ).limit(500)
    )).scalars().all()

    missing = 0
    for task in tasks:
        for eye in ("OD", "OS"):
            va = await db.scalar(
                select(M11PostopVa.id).where(
                    and_(
                        M11PostopVa.plan_id == task.plan_id,
                        M11PostopVa.eye_side == eye,
                        M11PostopVa.node_code == task.node_code,
                        M11PostopVa.is_deleted.is_(False),
                    )
                )
            )
            if va is None:
                continue
            exists = await db.scalar(
                select(M11PostopIndicator.id).where(
                    and_(
                        M11PostopIndicator.plan_id == task.plan_id,
                        M11PostopIndicator.eye_side == eye,
                        M11PostopIndicator.node_code == task.node_code,
                        M11PostopIndicator.is_deleted.is_(False),
                    )
                )
            )
            if exists is None:
                missing += 1
                from app.tasks.m11.postop_tasks import calc_indicators_task
                calc_indicators_task.delay(task.plan_id, eye, task.node_code)

    logger.warning("indicator_audit_job missing=%d", missing)
    return {"missing": missing}


async def reconcile_job(db: Any) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """对账：计划计数与任务聚合是否一致、依从率是否可复算。"""
    from app.models.m11.postop import M11PostopPlan, M11PostopTask

    plans = (await db.execute(
        select(M11PostopPlan).where(
            M11PostopPlan.is_deleted.is_(False),
        ).limit(1000)
    )).scalars().all()

    diffs = []
    for plan in plans:
        tasks = (await db.execute(
            select(M11PostopTask).where(
                and_(
                    M11PostopTask.plan_id == plan.id,
                    M11PostopTask.is_extra.is_(False),
                    M11PostopTask.is_deleted.is_(False),
                )
            )
        )).scalars().all()
        total = len(tasks)
        completed = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status == "COMPLETED")
        missed = sum(1 for t in tasks if t.status == "MISSED")
        if plan.planned_task_count != total:
            diffs.append({"plan_no": plan.plan_no, "type": "TASK_COUNT_DIFF",
                          "local": plan.planned_task_count, "calc": total})
        if plan.completed_task_count != completed:
            diffs.append({"plan_no": plan.plan_no, "type": "COMPLETED_DIFF",
                          "local": plan.completed_task_count, "calc": completed})
        if plan.missed_task_count != missed:
            diffs.append({"plan_no": plan.plan_no, "type": "MISSED_DIFF",
                          "local": plan.missed_task_count, "calc": missed})

    logger.warning("reconcile_job plans=%d diffs=%d", len(plans), len(diffs))
    return {"plans": len(plans), "diffs": len(diffs), "detail": diffs[:50]}


JOB_CONFIG = [
    {"id": "m11_postop_due_remind", "func": due_remind_job,
     "cron": "0 9 * * *", "name": "到期自动提醒"},
    {"id": "m11_postop_overdue_mark", "func": overdue_mark_job,
     "cron": "0 1 * * *", "name": "逾期任务标记"},
    {"id": "m11_postop_lost_judge", "func": lost_judge_job,
     "cron": "30 1 * * *", "name": "失访判定"},
    {"id": "m11_postop_indicator_audit", "func": indicator_audit_job,
     "cron": "0 4 * * *", "name": "指标缺失补算"},
    {"id": "m11_postop_reconcile", "func": reconcile_job,
     "cron": "20 4 * * *", "name": "计划与任务对账"},
]


def register_jobs(scheduler: Any, db_factory: Any, redis: Any) -> None:
    """把本子模块的定时任务注册到全局调度器。

    Args:
        scheduler: APScheduler 实例。
        db_factory: 异步会话工厂。
        redis: Redis 客户端。
    """
    for item in JOB_CONFIG:
        func = item["func"]

        async def _runner(f=func):
            async with db_factory() as db:
                return await f(db, redis)

        scheduler.add_job(
            _runner,
            trigger="cron",
            id=item["id"],
            name=item["name"],
            replace_existing=True,
            misfire_grace_time=900,
            coalesce=True,
            max_instances=1,
        )
    logger.info("m11-06 postop jobs registered: %d", len(JOB_CONFIG))
```
